  SUB-GROUP : Traffic Rules
  QUESTION  : What is the shape of prohibitory sign?
  ANSWER    : Round shap, two-layer surrounded edge - thick red inside edge, white thin outside edge- Inside sign generally is black.

  TOPIC 101 of 192

  ANSWER    : The vehicle from the right hand.
